</section><section data-mw-section-id="26" id="mwE9k"><h2 id="Explanatory_notes">Explanatory notes</h2>
<span class="mw-empty-elt" about="#mwt420" typeof="mw:Transclusion" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Notelist","href":"./Template:Notelist"},"params":{},"i":0}}]}' id="mwE9o"><style data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r1327269900" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" about="#mwt421" data-mw='{"name":"templatestyles","attrs":{"src":"Reflist/styles.css"}}'>.mw-parser-output .reflist-columns-2{column-width:30em}.mw-parser-output .reflist-columns-3{column-width:25em}body.skin-vector-2022 .mw-parser-output .reflist-columns-2{column-width:27em}body.skin-vector-2022 .mw-parser-output .reflist-columns-3{column-width:22.5em}.mw-parser-output .references[data-mw-group=upper-alpha]{list-style-type:upper-alpha}.mw-parser-output .references[data-mw-group=upper-roman]{list-style-type:upper-roman}.mw-parser-output .references[data-mw-group=lower-alpha]{list-style-type:lower-alpha}.mw-parser-output .references[data-mw-group=lower-greek]{list-style-type:lower-greek}.mw-parser-output .references[data-mw-group=lower-roman]{list-style-type:lower-roman}.mw-parser-output div.reflist-liststyle-upper-alpha .references{list-style-type:upper-alpha}.mw-parser-output div.reflist-liststyle-upper-roman .references{list-style-type:upper-roman}.mw-parser-output div.reflist-liststyle-lower-alpha .references{list-style-type:lower-alpha}.mw-parser-output div.reflist-liststyle-lower-greek .references{list-style-type:lower-greek}.mw-parser-output div.reflist-liststyle-lower-roman .references{list-style-type:lower-roman}</style></span><div about="#mwt420" id="mwE9s">
<div class="mw-references-wrap mw-references-columns" typeof="mw:Extension/references" about="#mwt422" data-mw='{"name":"references","attrs":{"group":"lower-alpha","responsive":"1"},"body":{"extsrc":""}}' id="mwE9w"><ol class="mw-references references" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" id="mwE90"><li about="#cite_note-8" id="cite_note-8" data-mw-footnote-number="a"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwE94"><a href="./List_of_capitals_in_the_United_States#cite_ref-8" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" rel="mw:referencedBy" id="mwE98"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwE-A">↑</span></a></span> <span id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-8" class="mw-reference-text reference-text" data-mw-group="lower-alpha">Convened May 10, 1775, prior to independence.</span></li>
<li about="#cite_note-12" id="cite_note-12" data-mw-footnote-number="b"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwE-E"><a href="./List_of_capitals_in_the_United_States#cite_ref-12" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" rel="mw:referencedBy" id="mwE-I"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwE-M">↑</span></a></span> <span id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-12" class="mw-reference-text reference-text" data-mw-group="lower-alpha">Extensive damage to the Pennsylvania State House during the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="./Philadelphia_campaign#Capture_of_Philadelphia" title="Philadelphia campaign" id="mwE-Q">British occupation of Philadelphia</a> necessitated this temporary meeting place.</span></li>
<li about="#cite_note-17" id="cite_note-17" data-mw-footnote-number="c"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwE-U"><a href="./List_of_capitals_in_the_United_States#cite_ref-17" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" rel="mw:referencedBy" id="mwE-Y"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwE-c">↑</span></a></span> <span id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-17" class="mw-reference-text reference-text" data-mw-group="lower-alpha">Congress was forced to move from Philadelphia due to a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="./Pennsylvania_Mutiny_of_1783" title="Pennsylvania Mutiny of 1783" id="mwE-g">mutiny carried out by disgruntled soldiers</a> of the Continental Army.</span></li>
<li about="#cite_note-18" id="cite_note-18" data-mw-footnote-number="d"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwE-k"><a href="./List_of_capitals_in_the_United_States#cite_ref-18" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" rel="mw:referencedBy" id="mwE-o"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwE-s">↑</span></a></span> <span id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-18" class="mw-reference-text reference-text" data-mw-group="lower-alpha">Government offices <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="./Relocation_of_the_United_States_Government_to_Trenton" title="Relocation of the United States Government to Trenton" id="mwE-w">were evacuated to Trenton, New Jersey</a>, from August to November 1799 following an outbreak of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="./Yellow_fever" title="Yellow fever" id="mwE-0">yellow fever</a> in Philadelphia.</span></li>
<li about="#cite_note-20" id="cite_note-20" data-mw-footnote-number="e"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwE-4"><a href="./List_of_capitals_in_the_United_States#cite_ref-20" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" rel="mw:referencedBy" id="mwE-8"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwE_A">↑</span></a></span> <span id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-20" class="mw-reference-text reference-text" data-mw-group="lower-alpha">The District of Columbia was formed February 27, 1801, with the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="./District_of_Columbia_Organic_Act_of_1801" title="District of Columbia Organic Act of 1801" id="mwE_E">District of Columbia Organic Act of 1801</a>.  The city of Washington was founded in 1791 and construction of the new capital began while it was still part of Maryland. President <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="./John_Adams" title="John Adams" id="mwE_I">John Adams</a> moved to the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="./White_House" title="White House" id="mwE_M">White House</a> on November 1, 1800, and the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="./6th_United_States_Congress" title="6th United States Congress" id="mwE_Q">6th United States Congress</a> held its first session in Washington on November 17, 1800.<sup about="#mwt130" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-carter-p139_19-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"carter-p139"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-carter-p139-19"}}'><a href="./List_of_capitals_in_the_United_States#cite_note-carter-p139-19" id="mwE_U"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwE_Y"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwE_c">[</span>15<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwE_g">]</span></span></a></sup></span></li>
<li about="#cite_note-22" id="cite_note-22" data-mw-footnote-number="f"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwE_k"><a href="./List_of_capitals_in_the_United_States#cite_ref-22" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" rel="mw:referencedBy" id="mwE_o"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwE_s">↑</span></a></span> <span id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-22" class="mw-reference-text reference-text" data-mw-group="lower-alpha">President <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="./James_Madison" title="James Madison" id="mwE_w">James Madison</a> fled to the home of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="./Caleb_Bentley" title="Caleb Bentley" id="mwE_0">Caleb Bentley</a> in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="./Brookeville,_Maryland" title="Brookeville, Maryland" id="mwE_4">Brookeville, Maryland</a> following the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="./Burning_of_Washington" title="Burning of Washington" id="mwE_8">burning of Washington</a> on August 24–25, 1814. As such, the town claims to have been the "U.S. Capital for a Day" despite the fact that Congress never met there.<sup about="#mwt141" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-21" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-21"}}'><a href="./List_of_capitals_in_the_United_States#cite_note-21" id="mwFAA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwFAE"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwFAI">[</span>16<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwFAM">]</span></span></a></sup></span></li>
<li about="#cite_note-Florida-45" id="cite_note-Florida-45" data-mw-footnote-number="g"><span rel="mw:referencedBy" class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwFAQ"><a href="./List_of_capitals_in_the_United_States#cite_ref-Florida_45-0" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" id="mwFAU"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwFAY">1</span></a> <a href="./List_of_capitals_in_the_United_States#cite_ref-Florida_45-1" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" id="mwFAc"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwFAg">2</span></a> <a href="./List_of_capitals_in_the_United_States#cite_ref-Florida_45-2" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" id="mwFAk"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwFAo">3</span></a> <a href="./List_of_capitals_in_the_United_States#cite_ref-Florida_45-3" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" id="mwFAs"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwFAw">4</span></a> <a href="./List_of_capitals_in_the_United_States#cite_ref-Florida_45-4" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" id="mwFA0"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwFA4">5</span></a> <a href="./List_of_capitals_in_the_United_States#cite_ref-Florida_45-5" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" id="mwFA8"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwFBA">6</span></a> <a href="./List_of_capitals_in_the_United_States#cite_ref-Florida_45-6" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" id="mwFBE"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwFBI">7</span></a> <a href="./List_of_capitals_in_the_United_States#cite_ref-Florida_45-7" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" id="mwFBM"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwFBQ">8</span></a></span> <span id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-Florida-45" class="mw-reference-text reference-text" data-mw-group="lower-alpha">The <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="./Spain" title="Spain" id="mwFBU">Spanish</a> name <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="./Spanish_Florida" title="Spanish Florida" id="mwFBY"><i id="mwFBc">La Florida</i></a> originally referred to all of the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="./North_America" title="North America" id="mwFBg">American continent</a> north of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="./Mexico" title="Mexico" id="mwFBk">Mexico</a>.  As other <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="./Europe" title="Europe" id="mwFBo">European</a> nations colonized North America, the extent of <i id="mwFBs">La Florida</i> shrank to encompass only the Spanish territorial claims in the southeastern portion of the present <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="./United_States" title="United States" id="mwFBw">United States</a>.</span></li>
<li about="#cite_note-Arkansas-49" id="cite_note-Arkansas-49" data-mw-footnote-number="h"><span rel="mw:referencedBy" class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwFB0"><a href="./List_of_capitals_in_the_United_States#cite_ref-Arkansas_49-0" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" id="mwFB4"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwFB8">1</span></a> <a href="./List_of_capitals_in_the_United_States#cite_ref-Arkansas_49-1" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" id="mwFCA"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwFCE">2</span></a> <a href="./List_of_capitals_in_the_United_States#cite_ref-Arkansas_49-2" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" id="mwFCI"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwFCM">3</span></a></span> <span id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-Arkansas-49" class="mw-reference-text reference-text" data-mw-group="lower-alpha">The name <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="./Arkansas" title="Arkansas" id="mwFCQ">Arkansas</a> has been pronounced and spelled in a variety of fashions.  The region was organized as the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="./Territory_of_Arkansaw" title="Territory of Arkansaw" class="mw-redirect" id="mwFCU">Territory of Arkansaw</a> on July 4, 1819, but the territory was admitted to the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="./United_States" title="United States" id="mwFCY">Union</a> as the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="./Arkansas" title="Arkansas" id="mwFCc">State of Arkansas</a> on June 15, 1836.  The name was historically pronounced <span class="rt-commentedText nowrap" id="mwFCg"><span class="IPA nopopups noexcerpt" lang="en-fonipa" id="mwFCk"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="./Help:IPA/English" title="Help:IPA/English" id="mwFCo">/<span style="border-bottom:1px dotted" id="mwFCs"><span title="/ˈ/: primary stress follows" id="mwFCw">ˈ</span><span title="/ɑːr/: 'ar' in 'far'" id="mwFC0">ɑːr</span><span title="'k' in 'kind'" id="mwFC4">k</span><span title="/ə/: 'a' in 'about'" id="mwFC8">ə</span><span title="'n' in 'nigh'" id="mwFDA">n</span><span title="'s' in 'sigh'" id="mwFDE">s</span><span title="/ɔː/: 'au' in 'fraud'" id="mwFDI">ɔː</span></span>/</a></span></span>, <span class="rt-commentedText nowrap" id="mwFDM"><span class="IPA nopopups noexcerpt" lang="en-fonipa" id="mwFDQ"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="./Help:IPA/English" title="Help:IPA/English" id="mwFDU">/<span style="border-bottom:1px dotted" id="mwFDY"><span title="/ɑːr/: 'ar' in 'far'" id="mwFDc">ɑːr</span><span title="/ˈ/: primary stress follows" id="mwFDg">ˈ</span><span title="'k' in 'kind'" id="mwFDk">k</span><span title="/æ/: 'a' in 'bad'" id="mwFDo">æ</span><span title="'n' in 'nigh'" id="mwFDs">n</span><span title="'z' in 'zoom'" id="mwFDw">z</span><span title="/ə/: 'a' in 'about'" id="mwFD0">ə</span><span title="'s' in 'sigh'" id="mwFD4">s</span></span>/</a></span></span>, and several other variants.  In 1881, the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="./Arkansas_General_Assembly" title="Arkansas General Assembly" id="mwFD8">Arkansas General Assembly</a> passed the following concurrent resolution (Arkansas Statutes, Title 1, Chapter 4, Section 105):
<blockquote id="mwFEA">Whereas, confusion of practice has arisen in the pronunciation of the name of our state and it is deemed important that the true pronunciation should be determined for use in oral official proceedings.</blockquote>
<blockquote id="mwFEE">And, whereas, the matter has been thoroughly investigated by the State Historical Society and the Eclectic Society of Little Rock, which have agreed upon the correct pronunciation as derived from history, and the early usage of the American immigrants.</blockquote>
<blockquote id="mwFEI">Be it therefore resolved by both houses of the General Assembly, that the only true pronunciation of the name of the state, in the opinion of this body, is that received by the French from the Native Americans and committed to writing in the French word representing the sound. It should be pronounced in three (3) syllables, with the final "s" silent, the "a" in each syllable with the Italian sound, and the accent on the first and last syllables. The pronunciation with the accent on the second syllable with the sound of "a" in "man" and the sounding of the terminal "s" is an innovation to be discouraged.</blockquote>
Citizens of the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="./Kansas" title="Kansas" id="mwFEM">State of Kansas</a> often pronounce the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="./Arkansas_River" title="Arkansas River" id="mwFEQ">Arkansas River</a> <span class="rt-commentedText nowrap" id="mwFEU"><span class="IPA nopopups noexcerpt" lang="en-fonipa" id="mwFEY"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="./Help:IPA/English" title="Help:IPA/English" id="mwFEc">/<span style="border-bottom:1px dotted" id="mwFEg"><span title="/ɑːr/: 'ar' in 'far'" id="mwFEk">ɑːr</span><span title="/ˈ/: primary stress follows" id="mwFEo">ˈ</span><span title="'k' in 'kind'" id="mwFEs">k</span><span title="/æ/: 'a' in 'bad'" id="mwFEw">æ</span><span title="'n' in 'nigh'" id="mwFE0">n</span><span title="'z' in 'zoom'" id="mwFE4">z</span><span title="/ə/: 'a' in 'about'" id="mwFE8">ə</span><span title="'s' in 'sigh'" id="mwFFA">s</span></span>/</a></span></span> in a manner similar to the common pronunciation of the name of their state.</span></li>
<li about="#cite_note-51" id="cite_note-51" data-mw-footnote-number="i"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwFFE"><a href="./List_of_capitals_in_the_United_States#cite_ref-51" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" rel="mw:referencedBy" id="mwFFI"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwFFM">↑</span></a></span> <span id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-51" class="mw-reference-text reference-text" data-mw-group="lower-alpha">Due to flooding in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="./Sacramento,_California" title="Sacramento, California" id="mwFFQ">Sacramento</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="./San_Francisco" title="San Francisco" id="mwFFU">San Francisco</a> served as a temporary capital from January 24, 1862 to May 15, 1862. <li about="#cite_note-53" id="cite_note-53" data-mw-footnote-number="j"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwFF8"><a href="./List_of_capitals_in_the_United_States#cite_ref-53" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" rel="mw:referencedBy" id="mwFGA"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwFGE">↑</span></a></span> <span id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-53" class="mw-reference-text reference-text" data-mw-group="lower-alpha">From December 3, 1859, to December 3, 1861, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="./History_of_Denver" title="History of Denver" id="mwFGI">Denver City</a> was formally the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="./History_of_Denver" title="History of Denver" id="mwFGM">City of Denver, Auraria, and Highland</a>.</span></li>
<li about="#cite_note-54" id="cite_note-54" data-mw-footnote-number="k"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwFGQ"><a href="./List_of_capitals_in_the_United_States#cite_ref-54" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" rel="mw:referencedBy" id="mwFGU"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwFGY">↑</span></a></span> <span id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-54" class="mw-reference-text reference-text" data-mw-group="lower-alpha">On November 15, 1902, the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="./History_of_Denver" title="History of Denver" id="mwFGc">City of Denver</a> became the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="./Denver" title="Denver" id="mwFGg">City and County of Denver</a>.</span></li>
<li about="#cite_note-64" id="cite_note-64" data-mw-footnote-number="l"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwFGk"><a href="./List_of_capitals_in_the_United_States#cite_ref-64" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" rel="mw:referencedBy" id="mwFGo"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwFGs">↑</span></a></span> <span id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-64" class="mw-reference-text reference-text" data-mw-group="lower-alpha">Note: The Louisiana Capitals information may be incorrect or incomplete. See <link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1333433106" about="#mwt356" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" data-mw='{"name":"templatestyles","attrs":{"src":"Module:Citation/CS1/styles.css"},"body":{"extsrc":""}}' id="mwFGw"/><cite class="citation web cs1" id="mwFG0"><a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://web.archive.org/web/20060615095136/http://www.state.la.us/about_history2.htm" class="external text" id="mwFG4">"Louisiana History"</a>. <li about="#cite_note-Charlestown-74" id="cite_note-Charlestown-74" data-mw-footnote-number="m"><span rel="mw:referencedBy" class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwFHM"><a href="./List_of_capitals_in_the_United_States#cite_ref-Charlestown_74-0" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" id="mwFHQ"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwFHU">1</span></a> <a href="./List_of_capitals_in_the_United_States#cite_ref-Charlestown_74-1" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" id="mwFHY"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwFHc">2</span></a></span> <span id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-Charlestown-74" class="mw-reference-text reference-text" data-mw-group="lower-alpha">The modern spelling of 'Charleston' did not take hold until the 1780s.<sup about="#mwt385" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-73" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-73"}}'><a href="./List_of_capitals_in_the_United_States#cite_note-73" id="mwFHg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwFHk"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwFHo">[</span>61<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwFHs">]</span></span></a></sup></span></li>

</div></td></tr><tr><th scope="row" class="navbox-group" style="width:1%"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="./Capital_city" title="Capital city">Permanent capital</a></th><td class="navbox-list-with-group navbox-list navbox-even hlist" style="width:100%;padding:0"><div style="padding:0 0.25em">

</div></td></tr></tbody></table></div>
